  • Implemented In production with CALLMARKER_ENABLED=true both CRMs are called in parallel and independently: a failure of one does not affect the other. Timeout — 15 s for each, no retries.
  • Implemented CallMarker success is only a 2xx status and the exact text “Customer has been added successfully”. HTTP 200 by itself is not considered success. The success text is the same for create and update. On timeout, delivery is uncertain.
  • Implemented On an error from either CRM or a duplicate — one combined email per submission to the user with ID 84 (send::emailListener, type CallMarker): first and last name, submitted and outgoing number, origin and source, environment, campaign ID and name (e.g. 13565 (בדיקות)), time in Asia/Jerusalem and UTC, and for each CRM — result, HTTP status, response body (up to 500 characters, token stripped) and explanation. All values are HTML-escaped; the email subject contains no customer data. Logs contain only the CRM, the reason and the status.
Reason in the emailExplanation
DUPLICATEBody contains “Duplicate customer”: the CRM rejected it as a duplicate; the data was not updated by this operation. Also observed for a number from another campaign.
INVALID_NUMBERBody contains “Invalid number format”: verify the number value, country and format.
HTTP_ERRORNon-2xx status (CallMarker or Kaveret): success not confirmed.
TIMEOUTNo response within 15 s: delivery is uncertain (the CRM may have processed the request); no retry.
NETWORK_ERRORNetwork error: delivery is uncertain; no retry.
NOT_CONFIGUREDNo CALLMARKER_TOKEN: nothing was sent.
PHONE_NOT_SENDABLEThe phone could not be normalized: CallMarker was not called.
UNEXPECTED_RESPONSEAny other response: success not confirmed.
INTERNALUnexpected exception during delivery: success not confirmed.

Accepted limitations (the user decided not to change them):

  • Accepted limitation The event is processed in the same process; this is not a durable queue: a restart before delivery loses the lead without an alert.
  • Accepted limitation A failure to fire the event is only logged; there is no fallback email.
  • Accepted limitation The existing EmailListener does not verify delivery statuses returned by the mail provider (and sends emails only on stage and in production).
  • Accepted limitation Sending an alert does not prove the email reached the mailbox.

Verification status:

  • Verified by live test Website: stage testing confirmed by the user; the operation of the whole deployed flow on stage was confirmed by the user overall. Individual results were not provided.
  • Open question A separate controlled verification of Contact Us from the +60 app on stage is not described in the documents.
  • Implemented Mock tests run by me on 2026-10-15 (without network, DB or .env): Contact Us — 34 of 34, customer test — 43 of 43, user sync — 67 of 67, subscription activation — 17 of 17.

3. Existing mediwho.com/new-client flow (migration context)

Existing flow This is an EXISTING Mediwho flow that must be assessed during the migration. It is not an implemented CallMarker flow: link generation on the CallMarker side and connecting to this path are not approved and not implemented. The Kaveret handover document referenced by the audit is missing from the repository, so this description is based on source code: frontend (/Users/Danil/WebstormProjects/frontend, commit 0b94d80c9) and API.

Synthetic example of a link with repeated ? (fictitious data):

https://mediwho.com/new-client?isNewClient=1?first_name=%D7%93%D7%A0%D7%94?last_name=%D7%9C%D7%95%D7%99?phone=0501234567?identity=012345674?birthday=17/05/1990?email=dana.levi@example.test

The same link with regular separators: …/new-client?isNewClient=1&first_name=…&last_name=…&phone=0501234567&identity=012345674&birthday=17/05/1990&email=dana.levi@example.test. The frontend parses both forms identically. Incoming parameters: first_name, last_name, phone, identity, birthday, email, isNewClient. The API expects the birth date in dd/MM/yyyy format.

StepWhat happens (per the code)
Parameter capturefrontend/src/pages/_app.js:20–30, 97–103. If the URL contains one of the marker strings (isNewClient among them) and parsing found at least one pair, all pairs are saved to authStore.serviceUrlDetails together with ref_url (the full URL). There is no dedicated /new-client route in the source code: the path falls into the generic PublicRouter (frontend/src/pages/routes.js:129), and the behavior is determined by the parameters.
URL parsingfrontend/src/utils/urlChecker.js:14–26, function getUrlParams. The regular expression [?&]+([^=&]+)=([^?&]*) treats both ? and & as separators, so a link with repeated ? is parsed the same way as one with regular &. Values go through decodeURIComponent; + is not turned into a space; an unescaped ? or & inside a value truncates it. Line 18 (url.replace(/^[?&]+/, '')) does not store its result and effectively does nothing. Standard parsing (URLSearchParams, the server, logs) would interpret a link with repeated ? differently: everything after the first ? would become a single value of the first parameter.
Agent not logged infrontend/src/pages/index.js:60–66 → myServicesUrlLogin (frontend/src/utils/urlChecker.js:62–68): if isNewClient is non-empty — redirect to the agent login /auth/login/agent/step1. The phone from the link is not prefilled on the login page when isNewClient is present (frontend/src/pages/AuthRouter/Login/index.js:28–31).
Agent logged inmyServicesUrl (frontend/src/utils/urlChecker.js:143–154): no agent or insurance_agent role → logout(history, ref_url); role present → /agent/client-list. The truthiness of the isNewClient string is evaluated, so an empty value (isNewClient=) does not pass this step.
Popup openingfrontend/src/pages/AgentRouter/ClientList/index.js:85–87, 113–115: if the isNewClient key is present, the agentAddClient popup opens with payload = {…link parameters, status: 'VALIDATE'}.
Prefillfrontend/src/components/Popup/AgentAddClient/index.js:67–120: when status === 'VALIDATE', the following are filled from the link: firstName and lastName (via safeDecode), registerIdentity, registerEmail, registerPhone, registerBirthday. For email, phone and identity an error flag is set first, until validation clears it. If a value matches the agent’s own data, the form substitutes the agent’s data (onFieldChange, lines 395–428).
Validationfrontend/src/forms/fieldsHelper.js: first and last name — required|string; identity — required|digits:9|identityExists; birth date — required; phone — required|phoneExists (on input only digits are kept, must start with 0 or 5, at most 10 digits); email — required|email|string|emailExists. The *Exists validators call POST agent/validate-field (frontend/src/forms/plugins.js).
User creationagentService.addClient → POST agent/reg-client (frontend/src/utils/api.js:134). Body: identity, email (lowercased), phone (phoneGenerator strips leading 0, + and 972), first_name, last_name, birthday, lng and, if needed, portfolio_manager. For a non-resident, POST agent/reg-non-resident-client is used with passport and country code; these fields are not filled from the link.
API processingstart/routes/agent.js:66 (auth, is:(agent)) → AgentController.regClient:1694. The birth date must be in dd/MM/yyyy format (AgentRegClient.validateBirthdayReg:61). If the identity matches the agent’s identity, the agent is registered as a customer. If an existing user (by identity or phone) with the customer_fly role is found, the data is merged, regExistClient is called, response 201. Otherwise AgentRegClient.regNewClient:109: email and phone duplicate validation, User.create (status INCOMPLETE, agent_id — the agent), an Invitation record (BLOCKED), event incompleteByAgent::user, response 201.
MyBusinessincompleteByAgent::user → Crm/Registration.createNewUserCrmByAgent: looks up a MyBusiness lead (api.mbapps.co.il, Accounts class) by PhoneNumber and Env. If there is no lead, creates one (id_client, phone, name, identity, email, birthday, agent, etc.); if a lead named CREATE_NEW_VISA_CAL is found, updates it. This method has no NODE_ENV condition; the environment is passed in the Env field.
KaveretNo customer snapshot is sent to Kaveret on this path: regClient, regNewClient and regExistClient do not call kaveretSetDataBeforeSend::user. Kaveret learns about the customer only from subsequent events, e.g. CREATE_SUBSCRIPTION when a subscription is placed (AgentController.sendPlusSubSms:1438), and only in prod.
After creationfrontend/src/components/Popup/AgentAddClient/index.js:197–250: new user (201) → product selection popup (selectProduct / subModeSwitcher); existing user (200) → existingUser message; already linked family member (202) → warning.

Migration observations: no middleware is declared on the agent/reg-non-resident-client route (start/routes/agent.js:72). Personal data (identity, phone, email) is passed in the link URL and may end up in browser history and logs. How Kaveret builds the link is not visible from the repository.

  • Implemented Creating a new customer by an agent (the regNewClient branch of AgentController.regClient) now fires callmarker::syncUser with context AGENT_CUSTOMER_CREATION (AgentController.js:1751) → campaign 13569. The other branches of this path (agent as a customer, existing Fly lead, non-resident) are not connected.
  • Open question CallMarker-side generation of /new-client links is not approved. If introduced: repeated ? work only thanks to the custom frontend parser; the format must be agreed.

4. Customer test endpoint

  • Implemented POST /call-marker/test/customer with user_id and dry_run (dry run by default); protected by auth + is:(admin or developer); 404 in production. Outside production the live send is blocked by the network policy (403 CALLMARKER_DISABLED before the token is read), and CALLMARKER_TEST_ENABLED=true cannot override it; the dry run remains available. The live tests below are historical and currently unavailable.
  • Implemented Files: CallMarkerController.js (HTTP validation, dry run, live send conditions), app/Modules/CallMarker.js (loadCustomer — reads the user and related records; sendCustomer — HTTP send), app/Utils/callMarkerCustomer.js (pure mapping). CONTRACT_FIELDS (formerly ALLOWED_FIELDS) describes the contract and is verified by a test, but does not filter fields at runtime.
  • Implemented The campaign is always CAMPAIGNS_LIST.TESTS_CAMPAIGN (13565); neither the environment nor a request parameter changes it. CALLMARKER_TEST_CAMPAIGN_ID is a legacy variable.
  • Implemented A dry run returns a preview: fields, omitted with reasons, warnings, ambiguities, phone, config_complete_for_live. This flag is kept for compatibility and equals Boolean(campaign): it reflects only the presence of a campaign (currently always true) and does not mean readiness for a live send.
  • Implemented A live send is a POST to the Simple Customers API (https://app.callmarker.com/api/simple/customers, form-urlencoded, 15 s timeout, no retries). Response: the HTTP status and CallMarker body as is, creation_confirmed: false.
ProtectionCodeState

5. Customer identification and campaigns

  • Confirmed decision CallMarker identifies the customer by phone number.
  • Confirmed decision crm_id stores users.id as reference information. A different number with the same crm_id creates another CRM row (confirmed by the user; not verified in the recorded tests).
  • Confirmed decision Updating customer data through one campaign changes the shared customer data in other campaigns as well (confirmed by the user). This must not be extended without evidence to statuses and agent assignments that belong to a specific campaign.
  • Verified by live test Resending with the same number updated the existing customer in campaign 13565; no duplicate appeared (test 4; tests 5–7 used the same number).
  • Verified by live test With ignore_duplicates=1, CallMarker responded “Duplicate customer” for a number that was already in another campaign (the user’s report). So the duplicate detection is not necessarily limited to the target campaign; this does not prove that a customer cannot belong to several campaigns.
  • Implemented The ignore_duplicates flag is set explicitly only in Contact Us; the customer test does not send it, and it is not added globally to requests. The code has no duplicate or retry protection of its own.
  • Open question Phone change and shared number are open questions; family member behavior is deferred.

6. Sync, campaigns and lifecycle checkboxes

  • Implemented Event callmarker::syncUser → CallMarkerListener.syncUser → CallMarker.processUserSync (start/events.js:89). Runs on stage and in production; in development there is no automatic external sending. The per-user queue lives in the memory of one Node process, not across pods; every run reloads MediWho data. The customer is first looked up through the read-only API v2 (by number and crm_id); on a conflict (CRM_ID_CONFLICT, PHONE_CHANGED, MULTIPLE_MATCHES, etc.) nothing is written and user 84 gets an email. Then one Simple API POST without ignore_duplicates, unique, remove, reset; a timeout leaves the result uncertain, and there are no retries.
  • Verified by live test The operation of the deployed flow on stage was confirmed by the user overall; there was no production verification.

Sync contexts (verified against the API code on 2026-10-18):

ContextWhere it fires and exceptionsCampaign (stage and production)source

  • Implemented Registration and Contact Us only promote the state and keep a more advanced or ambiguous classification. Agent registration and agent address do not invent a personal customer status.

  • Verified by live test Live test 2026-10-14 (customer 24625344, POST with campaign=13567 without unique): the Simple API moved the customer into the new campaign instead of adding a second one. A sync write can therefore take a customer out of its previous campaign.
  • Open question How to add an existing customer to a second campaign without moving it is a question for CallMarker support. Agents’ own subscriptions are now written without unique: memberships are preserved and checked after the write (section 6).
  • Open question unique is not a general campaign transfer solution: it can remove the customer from the agents campaign.
  • Open question Persistence of campaign membership and sync state is deferred; a campaign cannot be derived from a single status snapshot.

Reading campaign membership and a customer by ID through API v2 (read-only):

  • Verified by live test Verified live: GET /api/v2/customers with include=custom_fields,campaign_customers, fields[customers]=id,name,number,email,crm_id and custom_fields_format=map works. Active memberships are embedded as campaign_customers[]: membership ID, campaign_id, status/status_name and disabled; campaign names are not embedded. Only active memberships in non-deleted campaigns are embedded.
  • Verified by live test Active and disabled memberships with campaign names are read separately: GET /api/v2/campaign-customers with filter[customer_id]={id}, filter[disabled]=0,1, include=campaign and fields[campaign]=id,name.
  • Verified by live test Waiting is the customer’s status within the campaign, not the campaign’s activity status. The membership updated_at alone does not prove the exact time of removal from a campaign.
  • Open question According to the updated API specification, membership and custom field changes do not update the customer’s updated_at (not tested separately), so such changes cannot be detected through it.
  • Implemented In the code (commit ad3939070, later ported to api-prod/stage): the API v2 lookups embed campaign_customers. Memberships are used: neutral and restricted updates write to the existing membership of the campaign of the user’s classification and read back the complete membership set; card creation verifies the resulting memberships. The api-prod/stage port (commit cf19603ff, 2026-10-26) does not mean production deployment or verification.
  • Verified by live test Verified live: reading by the internal CallMarker ID without the number and crm_id parameters — GET /api/v2/customers/24622736 with include=custom_fields,campaign_customers and custom_fields_format=map returned a single customer object with both includes.
  • Open question Documented alternative — GET /api/v2/customers?filter[id]=24622736&include=custom_fields,campaign_customers&custom_fields_format=map; not tested live. filter[number] is a phone lookup: the value 24622736 returned an empty list.
  • Open question These are API v2 read capabilities. They do not establish that the Simple API can write by ID instead of phone, nor that a write preserves several campaigns: in the test of 2026-10-14 a write with another campaign moved the customer. That is why the agent flows verify memberships after the write (section 6).

Profile and admin editing

  • Implemented Existing PUT /user/user-data and PATCH /user/edit-info send the full fresh mapping after persisted mapped changes. fathers_name is included. Admin edits dispatch after persistence steps, including partial failures after successful writes. A genuine phone change uses separate number_change first; profile sync proceeds only after phone confirmation (or format-only handling). An existing bound card is updated; a proven-absent card is created with the full mapping (see below); family profiles stay deferred. No lifecycle checkboxes, source or Opportunity are sent. Campaign selection uses the user’s classification: own agent role → 13567; personal ACTIVE/BLOCKED/AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER → 13566; otherwise CANCELED → 13568; otherwise 13569. Exactly one existing eligible membership is required; unrelated memberships are allowed; an agent with eligible memberships in both agent and customer classifications remains ambiguous. Read-back compares identity, sent email, six checkboxes and the complete membership ID/campaign set; it does not compare names, birthday, fathers_name or HMO values. Changes are detected, not rolled back; no automatic resend. Birthday, fathers_name and Hebrew-only HMO values were stage confirmed by the user on 2026-10-26; no per-branch or production evidence is implied.

Customer custom-field availability: the browser runner reported all 75 then-existing fields set to Use in all campaigns, with all 75 reopened for verification. This is account configuration evidence, not proof of API writes; the later fields 17022/17023 are separately confirmed by the user on stage.

Admin agent info edit (PATCH /user/edit-agent-info):

  • Implemented UserController.editAgentInfo (admin or money; admin-panel → an agent page → the EditAgentInfo popup) uses the same single event callmarker::adminUserEdit and the same task in the user’s queue (processAdminUserEdit → runAdminUserEdit) as PATCH /user/edit-info. The event is fired once, after the handler steps have settled (user save, STO, invitation, legacy CRM, Kaveret), also when a later step answers 400 or throws after a successful save. Never on a 404, a validation error or a failed user save.
  • Implemented Trigger: an admin change of first_name, last_name, identity or email that was persisted — then the full fresh neutral mapping (ADMIN_PROFILE_UPDATED) is sent, reloaded from the database, never the request body. A genuine phone change is a separate number_change; profile data follow only after the phone is confirmed on the same card or was already applied. A formatting-only phone change sends no number_change.
  • Implemented No sync for: money-role requests, STO-only changes (sto_id, sto_status, terminal_name), agency_id-only changes, an unchanged save.
  • Implemented Campaign: the existing membership by the user’s classification (own agent role → 13567), then the membership-set read-back; no lifecycle checkboxes, source or opportunity writes. Family members stay deferred. Validation: IDENTITY_EXIST / EMAIL_EXIST, PHONE_INVALID, PHONE_EXIST (in any equivalent form) — in one 400 { errorArray }; the phone is stored as sent.
  • Verified by live test Confirmed by the user on stage on 2026-10-26 (“after check stage -> checked, work clean”). This is an overall confirmation: IDs, logs and per-case results were not provided; production was not verified.
  • Open question Limitations: a “pending confirmation” or “outcome unknown” phone result stops the data part (one email, no retry); there is no durable state, automatic retry or delayed reconciliation; names are not compared by the read-back. Known implementation inconsistency: the invitation block takes personal fields from the request body for any role, so a money request carrying them updates the invitation although the users row is not saved; CallMarker ignores it.
  • Open question PUT /agent/client-info is connected separately, for three fields only (see the block below).

Agent client info (PUT /agent/client-info, AGENT_CLIENT_PROFILE_UPDATED):

  • Implemented AgentController.updateClientInfo (website → the agent’s client → the HMO popup). Allowed only for the acting agent’s own customer or a customer whose parent belongs to that agent (the stored agent_id values are compared); otherwise 403/404 as before, before any write or event.
  • Implemented After a successful save only changed hmo_id, hmo_plan_id and fathers_name are tracked; one event is fired in the handler’s finally. Only changed, non-empty values are sent: HMO — custom_field_16874 (hmo) (stage — custom_field_16957), plan — custom_field_16875 (hmo_plan) (stage — custom_field_16958), father’s name — custom_field_17022 (stage — custom_field_17023).
  • Implemented HMO and plan are Hebrew catalog names only, without IDs; an unknown ID is omitted with a diagnostic. The father’s name comes from the edited user’s own record, trimmed. Empty values do not clear CRM values; with nothing to send there is no lookup and no POST.
  • Implemented The required number and campaign come from the existing bound card and its eligible existing membership. No full customer mapping, lifecycle checkboxes, source or opportunity. Family members stay deferred (FAMILY_PROFILE_PENDING_DECISION).
  • Implemented A genuine phone change saved by the same request blocks this sync (PROFILE_PHONE_CHANGE_UNCONFIRMED, one email); this route sends no number_change. A failed or missing baseline read skips CallMarker (AGENT_CLIENT_BASELINE_READ_FAILED / AGENT_CLIENT_BASELINE_MISSING) while the business operation runs as before.
  • Verified by live test Confirmed by the user on stage on 2026-10-26 (“checked in stage, work clean”). This is an overall confirmation: IDs, logs and per-case results were not provided; production was not verified.
  • Open question The read-back checks the card identity, memberships and checkboxes; the three values themselves are not compared automatically. Whether a POST without name/email leaves those fields unchanged is not verified separately. No durable state, automatic retry or deduplication. The unrestricted user.merge in the handler is an existing limitation, not fixed.
  • Open question GET /agent/clients: a request parameter can override the agent ID — a separate deferred security issue outside the CallMarker migration, not fixed.

Restricted updates (the shared neutral pipeline: fresh load, the user’s queue, API v2 checks, the existing membership of the user’s classification, membership and checkbox read-back, private emails; only the context’s changed, non-empty allowlisted fields; empty values never clear CRM values):

  • Implemented The event is fired once, after a successful save, in the handler’s finally — also after a later failure; never on a denial, unchanged values or a failed save. A deletion is sent only after the archive transaction commits; the lookup phone is captured before the archive and kept in memory only.
  • Implemented Agency and mentor: PUT /user/main-agent and PUT /agent/update-mentor fire no event; the fields refresh on the customer’s next relevant sync. Fan-out to all of an agent’s customers is deferred.
  • Open question The read-back compares identity, memberships and checkboxes, plus email and blocked_agent; the other values (HMO, identity, birthday, father’s name, address, agent fields) are not compared automatically.

Missing-card creation and bootstrap:

  • Implemented When both API v2 lookups (by number and crm_id) completed and found no card, a connected event of a living, independent user creates the card with the full mapping — also for restricted contexts. An existing card still gets only the allowlisted fields. A lookup error, incomplete paging, a conflict or an unbound lead never creates.
  • Implemented Campaigns and initial flags by classification: own agent role → 13567; personal ACTIVE / BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER → 13566 (is_customer); otherwise CANCELED → 13568 (is_former_customer); otherwise 13569 (is_registered_without_subscription).
  • Implemented An agent with a personal subscription gets one card in two campaigns: the first POST, then the card and its membership are verified through API v2, then a second request with only number and campaign; afterwards both memberships must be active. Whether the second request adds a membership or moves the card is not confirmed: a move is reported as CREATE_MEMBERSHIP_UNCONFIRMED and not repaired.
  • Implemented Phone change: with no card by crm_id, the old or the new number, the card is created with the current number (PHONE_CHANGE_BOOTSTRAP) and no number_change is sent. New relative: the independent family head’s card is created first (FAMILY_HEAD_BOOTSTRAP), then the family_details record is sent once.
  • Open question Never created: on a user deletion (USER_DELETED; deferred), for a deleted user and for a family member — there are no separate relative cards; relatives stay model-208 records.

New family member (family_details, a record in model 208 בני משפחה):

  • Implemented When a new relative is created through POST /agent/reg-family-member (AgentController.regFamilyMember) and POST /family (RegistrationController.regAnotherUser, except the isAgent branch), one POST goes to the experimental route POST https://app.callmarker.com/api/simple/customers/external-change with webhook=family_details. number is the family head’s phone (Israeli local form); f_name, id_name, birthday and Reference are the relative’s data (first and last name, identity, birthday, relationship); crm_id is the relative’s MediWho users.id. Reference from related_as: PARTNER → בן זוג, CHILD → ילד, PARENT → הורה; any other value is not sent (RELATION_UNKNOWN). The route was supplied by CallMarker support; it is not in the vendor documentation snapshots.
  • Verified by live test Confirmed by the user on stage on 2026-10-26 (“stage checked, all work”): new-relative creation through POST /agent/reg-family-member and POST /family. This is the user’s overall confirmation: tested users, record IDs, logs and per-branch results were not provided; not every negative case, captcha branch, relationship type or concurrency scenario was individually verified.
  • Implemented Create-only: existing-user invitations and their acceptance, relative edits and removals, and backfill are not connected. CallMarker support confirmed crm_id support and the fix for extra empty records (user’s report, 2026-10-25).
  • Open question Data received means accepted, not API-verified creation: model 208 cannot currently be read through the supported public API. There is no automatic retry and no durable deduplication — a repeated delivery can create a duplicate. Foreign family-head phones remain unsupported and unverified. Phone-change integration is a separate future task.
  • Open question family_details only creates a model-208 record: family purchases and other opportunity events are not connected to it; opportunity behavior for family members is deferred.

Admin phone change (PUT /user/admin/change-phone, number_change):

  • Implemented Only UserController.changePhone: after the new phone is saved, a separate request goes to the experimental route POST https://app.callmarker.com/api/simple/customers/external-change with webhook=number_change (name, number — the card’s old phone, new_number). An ordinary customer upload with the new number is never used for a phone change. The same existing card bound by crm_id is required; the route was supplied by CallMarker support and is not in the vendor documentation snapshots.
  • Verified by live test Confirmed by the user on stage on 2026-10-26 (“check done”). This is an overall confirmation: tested users, card IDs, logs and per-case results, including the negative checks, were not provided.
  • Implemented One attempt and one immediate verification by reading the card by ID. “Accepted but unconfirmed” and “outcome unknown” are never resent: the card must be checked manually, read-only.
  • Open question There is no persistent state, durable queue, delayed reconciliation, automatic retry or global sync pause (deferred by the user). Other phone-change routes do not notify CallMarker; PATCH /user/edit-info and PATCH /user/edit-agent-info are connected as described above.

7. Opportunities: purchase, cancellation, recovery and verified behavior

Implemented Opportunities are sent automatically in five contexts: SUBSCRIPTION_ACTIVATED — Purchased (campaign 13566), SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELED — a separate Not purchased with a reason ID (status 3, campaign 13568), SUBSCRIPTION_RECOVERED — a new Purchased with the note שחזור מנוי, SUBSCRIPTION_BLOCKED — Not purchased with reason 367, SUBSCRIPTION_UNBLOCKED — Purchased with the note הוסדר תשלום (the last three — campaign 13566). The opportunity fields are added to the same POST as the customer (without source). There is no separate “open, then close” request. Other contexts do not send opportunity fields. Verified by live test The operation of the flow on stage was confirmed by the user overall.

Purchase flow:

  1. Implemented A personal user_subscription transitions to ACTIVE (card or free payment, bank approval, activation from CARD_BLOCKED; the row was not ACTIVE when loaded) or a collective purchase is created.
  2. Implemented The event carries the owner’s user_id and the exact user_subscription_id.
  3. Implemented Queued processing per user reloads the data; agents’ own (non-AGENT) subscriptions are processed with their memberships preserved (see below).

    4. Own (non-AGENT) subscriptions of agents (agent / insurance_agent; the AGENT_MEMBERSHIP_PENDING_SUPPORT reason is no longer used):

    EventCampaignOpportunityNote
    Purchase13566Purchased 2Confirmed by the user on stage on 2026-10-29 (before the network policy; an overall message, “stage check done, all OK” in Russian, scope inferred from the accompanying task)
    Blocking13566Not purchased 3, reason 367live verification pending; stage network access disabled by current policy
    Unblocking13566Purchased 2, product_field_1060 = הוסדר תשלוםlive verification pending; stage network access disabled by current policy
    Cancellation13568Not purchased 3, the exact request’s reason or 378; existing 1060 / 1061 ruleslive verification pending; stage network access disabled by current policy
    Recovery13566Purchased 2, product_field_1060 = שחזור מנויlive verification pending; stage network access disabled by current policy
    • Implemented In all five: is_agent = "1"; active memberships are read before the write (unreadable — nothing is sent) and once after: 13567 and every earlier membership must stay with the same IDs, exactly one in the event’s campaign, nothing extra; otherwise one private “unconfirmed” email (AGENT_MEMBERSHIP_CHANGED / AGENT_MEMBERSHIP_INCOMPLETE).
    • Implemented No unique, membership removal, automatic retry or repair. The context’s own checks (owner, AGENT tariff, stale status, ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION_EXISTS, family members) are unchanged.
    • Implemented No card — the shared combined-role creation: the first POST to the event’s campaign with the Opportunity, the second only number + 13567; the Opportunity is sent once.
    • Open question Neutral profile updates of a combined-role card can still stop with CLASSIFICATION_AMBIGUOUS; the campaign choice is deferred.
  4. Implemented The row is validated (loadPurchase): owner, ACTIVE status, the tariff exists and is not AGENT. Otherwise nothing is written; email to user 84.
  5. Implemented Customer lookup and conflict checks via API v2; the customer’s opportunities are read before the write.
  6. Implemented Tariff → subscriptions.callmarker_product_id; customer and opportunity fields are prepared (buildOpportunityFields).
  7. Implemented One POST: customer + Purchased opportunity (if the mapping is ready).
  8. Implemented Post-write verification — read-only via API v2; no resend.

Current opportunity field mapping:

ParameterValueIf something is missing
product_idsubscriptions.callmarker_product_id of the tariff of the exact event row — the internal CallMarker product IDNo value or not a positive integer → PRODUCT_MAPPING_MISSING / PRODUCT_MAPPING_INVALID: the opportunity is not sent, the customer is written without opportunity fields, email to user 84

opportunity_not_purchased_reason_idOnly with status 3: blocking — 367; cancellation — by the exact request’s reason, otherwise 378Table below; Simple API acceptance is not verified

Manually added columns (no migration in the repository): subscriptions.callmarker_product_id — BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL, the internal product ID in the catalog of the configured CallMarker account (separate stage and production IDs are not always required: a shared catalog may use the same IDs). The product must be active, have an open price and be configured for campaign 13566, where the automatic purchase goes; browser inspection confirmed that 7794 is associated with test campaign 13565, while association with 13566 is not explicitly confirmed — that campaign’s configuration must be checked. The successful stage verification is confirmed by the user; the configuration changes behind it are not recorded here. users.callmarker_agent_name — VARCHAR(255) NULL: the exact CallMarker user Name or that user’s configured CRM Name; confirmed live for the Name “Yuval Kopel REP”, while CRM Name matching and identity verification are limited — API v2 exposes only the user id and Name. The SQL artifact provided 137 “tariff → CallMarker product” mappings, e.g. subscriptions.id 192 / SKU I-0004 → 7794. This documentation did not query or verify them individually.

  • Implemented Do not confuse: the customer’s custom_field_16876 (active_product_id) is the MediWho products.id; the opportunity product_id is the internal CallMarker product ID; opportunity_crm_id is the tariff; product_field_1059 is the specific purchase row.
  • Implemented Missing or invalid product mapping, or an invalid price → the customer is written without opportunity fields, and user 84 gets one “opportunity skipped” email. No provider, provider name, product name or callmarker_agent_name → only that value is skipped. If the write itself failed or timed out, the regular sync email notes that the opportunity fields were in the request and the result is unknown.

Cancellation (SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELED, campaign 13568 FORMER_CUSTOMERS_CAMPAIGN):

  • Implemented Cancellation from ACTIVE, BLOCKED or AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER to CANCELED in three connected places: manual cancellation (canceledSubscription), Galil cancellation (cancelUserSubscription_Galil) and scheduled future cancellation execution (FutureCancellationRunner.runOne; cancels ACTIVE only). The original status is captured before the CANCELED write; the tariff is not AGENT. The event carries cancellation_request_id — the request the operation itself used (Galil, scheduled cancellation); manual cancellation passes no request. A dispatch failure does not change the cancellation.
  • Implemented At run time: a family member — skipped with FAMILY_MEMBER_PENDING_DECISION; the row must exist, belong to the user and still be CANCELED (otherwise an email to user 84); if the user already has an ACTIVE personal subscription — skipped with ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION_EXISTS, so a stale event never moves the customer back.
  • Implemented Customer: campaign 13568, is_former_customer = "1", is_customer, is_lead and is_registered_without_subscription = "0", custom_field_16879 (is_blocked) = "0"; custom field IDs are chosen by environment (“(S)” on dev/stage). is_agent and is_test are not sent and not changed; no source.
  • Implemented A separate Not purchased opportunity in the same POST: opportunity_status = "3", product and price from the exact canceled row, opportunity_not_purchased_reason_id by the request’s reason (table below) or 378, opportunity_agent_name when configured, fields 1057–1059 as for a purchase. product_field_1060 is not sent. Status 3 here is a new record, not a cancellation of an existing sale.
  • Implemented Reason. The request is only the one in the event; it is read server-side and must reference this user_subscriptions row and its owner (requested_for). The latest request is never substituted. The stored request_reason is mapped by the table; the caller never supplies a reason. No request (CANCELLATION_REQUEST_UNKNOWN; manual cancellation — always), request not found (CANCELLATION_REQUEST_NOT_FOUND), another row or owner (CANCELLATION_REQUEST_MISMATCH), an empty (NOT_PURCHASED_REASON_MISSING) or unmapped reason (NOT_PURCHASED_REASON_UNMAPPED; UPGRADED is not an alias for UPGRADE) → 378 (לא ידוע).
  • Implemented The request read is optional: on failure — 378 with the diagnostic CANCELLATION_REQUEST_READ_FAILED; the customer and opportunity sync is not aborted.
  • Implemented Collection note (product_field_1061) — only on a cancellation from BLOCKED or AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER, from the same verified request with reason RETURNED_CHARGE, DENIAL_OF_TRANSACTION or BLOCKING_PAYMENT_METHOD: the msg of the request’s first message, text as stored (Hebrew and line breaks kept, no truncation and no author data). An unknown, missing, foreign or unreadable request, another reason, an empty or malformed first message → 1061 is omitted (COLLECTION_NOTE_MESSAGE_MISSING / COLLECTION_NOTE_MESSAGE_INVALID); the cancellation continues. In practice the note comes from Galil cancellations.
  • Open question After a cancellation there is no active product: custom_field_16876 (active_product_id) and custom_field_16877 (active_product_name) are omitted, not cleared, so the previous value may remain in the CRM — a clearing method is not agreed or tested. custom_field_16899 (roles) is recomputed by the snapshot (e.g. לקוח לשעבר).

Not purchased reason IDs (CALLMARKER_NOT_PURCHASED_REASONS, CALLMARKER_BLOCKING_REASON, CALLMARKER_UNKNOWN_REASON in app/Constants/Enums.js). One CallMarker account catalog: the IDs do not depend on the environment and are not custom field IDs. Simple API acceptance has not been tested.

Blocking and unblocking (SUBSCRIPTION_BLOCKED / SUBSCRIPTION_UNBLOCKED, campaign 13566 CUSTOMERS_CAMPAIGN):

  • Implemented BLOCKED and AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER mean custom_field_16879 (is_blocked) = "1"; CARD_BLOCKED alone does not set it. A customer with a blocked subscription stays a customer.
  • Implemented Blocking — ACTIVE → BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER, and CANCELED → BLOCKED (recovery with blocked_cancel): is_customer = "1", the other three — "0", is_blocked = "1"; a separate Not purchased opportunity ("3") with reason 367 (חסימת מנוי); no product_field_1060 or product_field_1061.
  • Implemented Unblocking — BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER → ACTIVE through an administrative status update (not a purchase): is_customer = "1", the other three — "0", is_blocked = "0"; a separate Purchased opportunity ("2") with product_field_1060 = הוסדר תשלום; opportunity_not_purchased_reason_id is not sent.
  • Implemented Not synced: a move between BLOCKED and AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER, an unchanged status, CARD_BLOCKED and other changes. Bank approval (AWAITING_BANK_APPROVAL → ACTIVE through updateBankApprovalStatus; the admin panel calls it for AWAITING_BANK_APPROVAL rows, the method itself does not restrict the original status, and the event is sent when the row was not ACTIVE) and activation from CARD_BLOCKED stay purchases (SUBSCRIPTION_ACTIVATED). Unblocking is BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER → ACTIVE through updateUserSubscriptionStatus (SUBSCRIPTION_UNBLOCKED, note הוסדר תשלום).
  • Implemented At run time the row is reloaded: if it is no longer in the expected status (SUBSCRIPTION_NOT_BLOCKED / SUBSCRIPTION_NOT_ACTIVE) or the tariff is AGENT, the event is stale and skipped (log only).
  • Implemented custom_field_16879 (is_blocked) in every full sync is CallMarker’s own calculation (blockingSubscriptionsQuery); the Kaveret snapshot flag (BLOCKED only) is not used.

Recovery (SUBSCRIPTION_RECOVERED, campaign 13566 CUSTOMERS_CAMPAIGN):

  • Implemented Only userSubscriptionRecovery (PUT /subscription-recovery): the row was CANCELED when loaded, is saved as ACTIVE, the tariff is known and not AGENT, the subscription is an ordinary personal one. A restoration to BLOCKED (CANCELED → BLOCKED) goes as a blocking, without a recovery opportunity. Users with their own agent role, family members and Fly/RDG are out of scope.
  • Implemented Customer: is_customer = "1", the other three lifecycle flags — "0"; is_agent is not cleared, is_test is not sent.
  • Implemented A new Purchased opportunity: opportunity_status = "2", product_field_1060 = שחזור מנוי; the same mapping for product, price, the optional configured agent and fields 1057–1059; no reason is sent. Previous opportunities are intentionally not modified; product_field_1061 is not used.
  • Implemented Not to be confused with unblocking: recovery is CANCELED → ACTIVE through PUT /subscription-recovery, note שחזור מנוי; unblocking is BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER → ACTIVE through a status update, note הוסדר תשלום and is_blocked = "0".

Shared safeguards (cancellation, recovery, blocking and unblocking):

  • Implemented The Simple API closes an open opportunity for the product instead of creating a separate record. So the existing customer’s opportunities are read before the write: an open (status 1) opportunity with this product_id → OPEN_OPPORTUNITY_EXISTS; the list could not be read → OPEN_OPPORTUNITY_UNKNOWN.
  • Implemented In both cases all opportunity fields are omitted, the customer campaign and checkboxes are still written, and user 84 gets one email; nothing is closed and there is no automatic retry. An open opportunity for another product does not block.
  • Implemented A missing or invalid product mapping or price also prevents opportunity delivery; the customer write is kept and user 84 gets an email.
  • Implemented Ordinary purchase behavior is unchanged: the guard is not applied to it; with an open opportunity, a purchase closes it, and post-write verification reports VERIFY_PRE_EXISTING_ONLY.
  • Implemented The agent and family-member exclusions and the trigger coverage are unchanged.
  • Open question The read before the write is not atomic: it does not protect against concurrent changes and does not prevent all duplicates.

Confirmed by the user on stage (before blocking and reason IDs were added; not tests performed while preparing this documentation; individual customers, times and requests were not provided):

  • Verified by live test Cancellation: campaign 13568 and the lifecycle flags were verified. Coverage of all three cancellation entry points is not confirmed.
  • Verified by live test A cancellation creates a new Not purchased opportunity.
  • Verified by live test A recovery creates a new Purchased opportunity with the recovery note (product_field_1060).
  • Verified by live test Previous opportunities remain unchanged.

  • Open question Manual cancellation sends 378; a request-linked cancellation sends its reason’s ID.
  • Open question Blocking: 13566, is_customer(S) = "1", is_blocked(S) = "1", Not purchased with reason 367.
  • Open question Unblocking: is_blocked(S) = "0", Purchased with the note הוסדר תשלום.
  • Open question Cancellation from BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER: 13568, is_blocked(S) = "0", the correct product_field_1061.
  • Open question Recovery still carries the note שחזור מנוי; previous opportunities are not changed in any of these scenarios.
  • Open question The collective purchase (13566, is_customer(S) = "1", Purchased with the purchase price, product_field_1059 = the new user_subscriptions.id).
  • Open question custom_field_16899 (roles) as plain text.

  • Open question Remaining profile scenarios: production, concurrent membership changes and untested negative branches. Stage field-value checks were confirmed by the user on 2026-10-26.

Post-write verification (read-only, bounded, no resend): before the write and once after it, GET /api/v2/opportunities?filter[customer_id]=…&include=user,product_fields&product_fields_format=map is read (all pages, at most 10 × 100). A match is product_id + product_field_1059 = the exact user_subscriptions.id; exactly one new such opportunity with the sent status (2 — purchase, recovery, unblocking; 3 — cancellation, blocking) and the sent values, including the reason ID (API v2 field not_purchased_reason_id; not yet observed live), 1060 and 1061 when sent → confirmed. Otherwise user 84 gets an “opportunity unconfirmed” email (VERIFY_ABSENT, VERIFY_AMBIGUOUS, VERIFY_MISMATCH, VERIFY_PRE_EXISTING_ONLY, VERIFY_LOOKUP_FAILED, etc.). HTTP 200 and “Customer has been added successfully” do not confirm the opportunity.

Agent assessmentMeaning
CONFIRMEDThe returned user Name equals callmarker_agent_name — a confirmed match by Name. If a CRM Name different from the Name is configured, the result is IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED.
AGENT_NOT_ASSIGNEDThe opportunity has no user — no assignment; email to user 84.
IDENTITY_UNVERIFIEDA user with a different Name is assigned; the configured value may be that user’s CRM Name. The opportunity is confirmed, but it is not proven that this is the correct agent (API v2 does not return the CRM Name).
NOT_SENTcallmarker_agent_name is not set — no name was sent.

Verified behavior (historical controlled tests on stage, 2026-10-14/15, test customer 24622736 in campaign 13565; separate from the automatic flow):

What was verifiedResult
An internal CallMarker product ID is required7794 created an opportunity; user_subscriptions.id, subscriptions.id and the table row number did not
Direct creation as PurchasedWorks
Agent assignmentLive matching was confirmed for the Name “Yuval Kopel REP” (CallMarker agent 13619). Unknown or unmatched names did not prevent closing, but the assignment stayed empty. CRM Name matching was not tested
Closing an open opportunityOpened → Purchased and Opened → Not purchased updated the same open opportunity

Historical “open, then close” sequence (the user’s report):

#RequestResult
1POST: product_id=7794, opportunity_crm_id=192, opportunity_price=90Created opportunity 1441001: status 1 / Opened, price 90, campaign_id = null, user_id = null.
2Next POST: the same + opportunity_status=2, opportunity_agent_name=אבישי קסThe same opportunity 1441001: status 2 / Purchased, campaign_id = 13565, user_id = null; no additional opportunity appeared.
  • Implemented Status 3 is not a cancellation of an existing sale: without an open opportunity it creates a new closed record. This is how a cancellation creates a separate Not purchased opportunity; the existing Purchased one is not changed.
  • Open question There is no permanent duplicate protection — an explicit decision by the user (deferred). Without an open opportunity, every Purchased POST creates a new opportunity; repeated or concurrent events, two pods during a rollout, or a manual resend after a timeout can create duplicates. The status-order validation suppresses only sequential repeated callbacks. Repeated cancellation, recovery, blocking or unblocking events can create additional closed opportunities as well. product_field_1059 is a correlation with the subscription row, not an enforced unique key (all opportunities of the same row share it); it helps find duplicates but does not prevent them. Payment idempotency is deferred as well.
  • Open question Creating Purchased without opportunity_agent_name has not been verified by a live test.

Agent configuration in CallMarker (for users.callmarker_agent_name):

  1. Proposed step Identify the correct existing CallMarker Agent user.
  2. Proposed step Write its exact Name or its configured CRM Name to users.callmarker_agent_name. Only Name matching is confirmed live; a CRM Name cannot be checked through API v2, and the result is IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED when it differs from the Name.
  3. Proposed step Do not associate a MediWho agent with an unrelated CallMarker user; if no suitable user exists, creating one is a separate administrative decision.
  4. Proposed step Validate the assignment through the opportunity user_id (API v2) and in the dashboard; do not treat IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED as a proven match.

Do not replay payment callbacks as a test: payment and commission idempotency is unresolved.

8. Field mapping

All custom fields are optional; the full set is sent by the customer test and user sync, while Contact Us sends only env and the lifecycle checkboxes. The first column shows the production ID and the “(S)” ID for dev/stage. Empty values are not sent. Full description in the cards of sections 4 and 5. The “Live test” column distinguishes: included and accepted (the field was in the request and CallMarker responded with success) and value verified in the UI (marked ✓).

  • Confirmed decision Business rule: a customer is expected to have only one ACTIVE subscription. There is no database constraint.
  • Implemented If a customer has several own ACTIVE user_subscriptions rows with different products, the snapshot rows disagree: all six snapshot keys (including 16876/16877) are omitted as AMBIGUOUS, the user sync writes nothing (an AMBIGUOUS_SOURCE_DATA email), the customer test answers 409. Only rows with user_id = users.id are considered, without family membership.

13. Recommended steps

  1. Proposed step Decide when and how to verify the implemented flow in production; a successful stage verification does not replace it.

  3. Proposed step Separately approve the rule for replacing unclear checkboxes on purchase.
  4. Proposed step Get from CallMarker support a way to add a customer to a second campaign without moving it; until then purchase, cancellation and recovery for agents stay skipped.
  5. Open question The collective purchase and custom_field_16899 (roles) as plain text: live verification pending; stage network access disabled by current policy. Stage must not be enabled through NODE_ENV=production; how to verify live is the user’s decision.
  6. Proposed step Agree on clearing custom_field_16876 (active_product_id) / custom_field_16877 (active_product_name) after a cancellation.
  7. Open question Blocking, unblocking, reason IDs 367–378 (except the confirmed 371) and the 1061 note: live verification pending; stage network access disabled by current policy (list in section 7).
  8. Proposed step Business choice: which campaign a neutral update uses when an agent is active in both 13567 and 13566 (now CLASSIFICATION_AMBIGUOUS, nothing written; no priority is invented).
  9. Proposed step A rapid chain of phone changes (a second change before the first is applied) fails (PHONE_MISMATCH / PHONE_SUPERSEDED); persistent state is deferred. unique is not used; no automatic retries or deduplication; model 208 is create-only; no automatic verification of every field.
  10. Proposed step When the team decides, return to permanent opportunity duplicate protection and sync-state persistence.
  11. Proposed step Connect the remaining registration branches and decide the behavior for family members, phone changes through other routes (except PUT /user/admin/change-phone) and shared numbers.
  12. Proposed step Close the questions on Selectbox (1/0, env texts), text length limit and foreign phones.

Do not use payment callbacks as a test method: payment and commission idempotency is unresolved.

Parameters: 99

1. Purpose and scope

CallMarker is to gradually replace Kaveret as the CRM that receives Mediwho customer data. MyBusiness remains and keeps working unchanged. The Kaveret path (app/Modules/Kaveret.js, which on prod sends the Kaveret.scopeGetUserInfo snapshot to a Make webhook) is not changed; the CallMarker test uses the shared snapshot only for six computed values.

  • Now: the customer test endpoint; Contact Us (website and +60 app); user sync on agent registration, customer creation, agent address, personal subscription activation (together with the automatic Purchased opportunity), cancellation (a separate Not purchased opportunity with a reason ID), recovery (a new Purchased opportunity), blocking and unblocking. See the overview.
  • Not implemented: other status transitions (CARD_BLOCKED and AWAITING_BANK_APPROVAL → CANCELED, expiry), manual activation of other statuses, import and administrative paths, Fly/RDG, the remaining registration branches, family members, persistent duplicate protection for opportunities, storing campaign membership, appointments, inbound messages.
  • Secrets: the token is set manually in CALLMARKER_TOKEN. This page shows only the placeholder <CALLMARKER_TOKEN>.

2. How to read this page

Each custom field is written as the exact API key with the business name in parentheses, for example custom_field_16893 (parent_agency_name). Request bodies contain only the exact key. A parameter card has rows, and the colored categories are strictly separated:

Documented API behavior — only what the provided CallMarker document says.
Current implementation — what the code in the API repository does now, based on reading the files.
Live test result — what is recorded in the 2026-10-11 test log and in user reports (tests 1–7); not reproduced here. Distinguished: field included in the request, request accepted, value verified in the UI.
Confirmed intended behavior — Mediwho business decisions. Implemented and verified only where the rows above say so.
Open questions / required validation — unresolved questions and validations against the real CRM.

Labels

Plan labelMeaningCode labelMeaning
Use nowPart of the current customer scope.Code matches decisionThe code follows the confirmed decision (based on reading the code).
Considered laterConsidered for the next stage.Code differsThe code does something other than what is confirmed or documented.
Not used nowUnderstood, but not used now.Only when configuredSent only when an optional setting is configured.
DeferredThe whole block is deferred.Temporary modeA temporary solution in the code until the next CRM test.
Decision pendingA mapping or mode has been proposed but not decided.Not implementedNo code, although the parameter is proposed or needed.
Not sent (as planned)Not sent, and this matches the plan.

General sending rules: missing optional values are omitted rather than sent empty; all values are sent as strings (leading zeros and a meaningful 0 are preserved). Whether omission or an empty value clears stored data is unknown. “If set” flags are not sent when not needed.

3. How the test endpoint works

Files: start/routes/call-marker.js (route), app/Controllers/Http/CallMarkerController.js (HTTP validation, dry run, live send conditions), app/Modules/CallMarker.js (data reading, HTTP sending, Contact Us), app/Utils/callMarkerCustomer.js (pure mapping and transformations), tests/callmarker-customer-regressions.js. The route is registered in start/routes/index.js.

  1. Route. POST /call-marker/test/customer, protected by auth + is:(admin or developer) (start/routes/call-marker.js:9).
  2. Environment guard. If NODE_ENV is neither development nor stage, the controller responds 404 NOT_FOUND. The env value sent to CallMarker does not replace this guard.
  3. Input. user_id must match ^\d{1,10}$ and be > 0, otherwise 400 INVALID_USER_ID. dry_run: absent, empty, true or 1 — dry run; false or 0 — live; otherwise 400 INVALID_DRY_RUN.
  4. Loading (read-only). CallMarker.loadCustomer(): the users row (missing — 404 USER_NOT_FOUND), the parent, the resolved agent (own, otherwise the parent’s agent), the agency user by agent.parent_agency_id, the customer’s own ACTIVE user_subscriptions rows with subscriptions.name, plus the shared Kaveret snapshot. The shape of the raw result is validated (readRawRows): unexpected — 500 UNEXPECTED_RAW_RESULT/UNEXPECTED_RAW_ROW, empty snapshot — 500 SNAPSHOT_MISSING.
  5. Mapping. buildCustomerPayload() returns the allowed fields, omitted[{field, label, reason}], ambiguities, warnings and phone{source, warnings, sent_format}. Omission reasons: EMPTY, NOT_USED, PENDING_CONFIRMATION, SOURCE_ROW_NOT_FOUND, AMBIGUOUS, INVALID_JSON, INVALID_SHAPE, INVALID_DATE, SELECT_MAP_NOT_CONFIGURED, SELECT_VALUE_NOT_MAPPED. A phone error yields 422.
  6. Campaign. Always CAMPAIGNS_LIST.TESTS_CAMPAIGN = 13565; neither the environment nor the request changes it. config_complete_for_live = Boolean(fields.campaign) is kept for compatibility: it reflects only the presence of a campaign (currently always true) and does not mean readiness for a live send — the token, the enable flag and ambiguities are verified only on the live path.
  7. Dry run. 200 {dry_run:true, user_id, url, content_type, fields, omitted, ambiguities, phone, warnings, config_complete_for_live}. No HTTP request is made and the response contains no token, but it does contain the customer’s personal data.
  8. Live send conditions. First the network policy: only NODE_ENV=production + CALLMARKER_ENABLED=true, and the endpoint answers 404 in production, so the live mode is currently unavailable everywhere (403 CALLMARKER_DISABLED); CALLMARKER_TEST_ENABLED=true cannot override it. The dry run remains available. Then (historical condition) CALLMARKER_TEST_ENABLED must be exactly true, otherwise 403 CALLMARKER_TEST_DISABLED. Non-empty ambiguities → 409 AMBIGUOUS_SOURCE_DATA. No token → 503 CALLMARKER_NOT_CONFIGURED.
  9. HTTP transport. CallMarker.sendCustomer: a single form-urlencoded POST to the fixed URL https://app.callmarker.com/api/simple/customers; token is added only here; 15 s timeout; any HTTP status is accepted; no retries. The customer test does not send the source and ignore_duplicates flags — they exist only in Contact Us.
  10. Response handling. Any CallMarker status → our 200 {dry_run:false, upstream:{status, body}, creation_confirmed:false, …}; the body is kept as is, the token is stripped. No response → 502 with TIMEOUT or NETWORK_ERROR. Exception → 500 INTERNAL_ERROR. Only error codes are logged. CallMarker may respond with HTTP 200 and an error text (“Invalid number format”), so the body must be inspected.

Environment variables (names only)

VariablePurpose
NODE_ENV + CALLMARKER_ENABLEDThe global switch: CallMarker requests only with production + true (raw values, strict); disabled by default; a change needs a restart.
CALLMARKER_TEST_ENABLEDThe second condition of the live test; cannot override the global switch.
CALLMARKER_TOKENSecret Simple API token (write); the user sets it manually. Read only on live paths.
CALLMARKER_ACCESS_TOKENSecret read-only Bearer token for API v2: user sync, Contact Us lookup, opportunity verification.
CALLMARKER_TEST_CAMPAIGN_IDHistorical: this is how the campaign was set in the 2026-10-11 tests. Not read since 2026-10-12; the campaign is a constant from CAMPAIGNS_LIST.
CALLMARKER_IS_DELETED_MAP, CALLMARKER_IS_BLOCKED_MAPNo longer used (since 2026-10-14): custom_field_16878 (is_deleted) and custom_field_16879 (is_blocked) are sent as "1"/"0".

.env.example no longer exists in the repository; placeholders are given in callmarker_instructions.md. For stage, the variables are set in Azure Key Vault.

4. Customers: standard parameters

The body encoding is not documented (form-urlencoded is a test assumption that works according to the log; files require multipart). Top-level env is not supported (ignored in tests 4–5) and has been replaced by the custom_field_16901 (env) field. Custom fields are in section 5.

envFormer top-level environment parameter. Not supported; replaced by the field custom_field_16901 (env).Not used nowNot sent (as planned)

Parameter details

token

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Authorization token of the CallMarker account.
Required / type
Required (per the documentation). String. Secret.
Mediwho source
Server setting CALLMARKER_TOKEN. The user sets it manually; the value never appears in documentation, dry runs, responses, or logs.
Transformation and sending rules
No transformation. Added only by the transport at send time.
Documented API behavior
Described as “unique authorization token”, required. The documentation does not specify where to pass it (body or query string).
Current implementation
CallMarker.sendCustomer adds token (Simple API, writes) to the form-urlencoded body. API v2 reads (user sync, the Contact Us lookup, opportunity verification) use a separate CALLMARKER_ACCESS_TOKEN (Bearer): API v2 rejects the Simple API token (401). The token is never shown in responses; an echo is replaced with [REDACTED].
Live test result 2026-10-11
Successful responses “Customer has been added successfully” according to the test log in callmarker_instructions.md and the user reports: a token in the form-urlencoded body is accepted.
Confirmed intended behavior
Taken from CALLMARKER_TOKEN; examples contain only the placeholder <CALLMARKER_TOKEN>.
Open questions / required validation
Record the response for a missing or invalid token. Keep the token out of logs.

campaign

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
The CallMarker campaign the customer is added to.
Required / type
Required (per the documentation). CallMarker campaign ID (number).
Mediwho source
CAMPAIGNS_LIST constants in app/Constants/Enums.js (IDs are strings). Customer test: always TESTS_CAMPAIGN = 13565 (בדיקות). Contact Us: stage uses 13565, production uses LEADS_CAMPAIGN = 13558. The CALLMARKER_TEST_CAMPAIGN_ID variable is historical (it set the campaign in the 2026-10-11 tests) and is no longer read.
Transformation and sending rules
No transformation.
Documented API behavior
The documented key is campaign: “campaign id to add customers to”.
Current implementation
Customer test: campaign = CAMPAIGNS_LIST.TESTS_CAMPAIGN.key (CallMarkerController.js:63); neither an environment variable nor a request parameter changes it. The flag config_complete_for_live = Boolean(fields.campaign) (line 77) is kept for compatibility and reflects only that a campaign is present; with the constant it is always true and does not mean readiness for a live send (the enable flag, the token, and ambiguities are checked only on the live path). Contact Us selects the campaign by environment (contactUsCampaign in app/Modules/CallMarker.js). campaign_id is not sent.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Customer added to campaign 13565 according to the test log in callmarker_instructions.md and the user reports.
Confirmed intended behavior
Customer test: 13565 (בדיקות). Contact Us in production: 13558 (לידים). User sync (stage and production): agent registration and agent address — 13567; user registration on the website and by an agent — 13569; personal subscription activation, recovery, blocking and unblocking — 13566; cancellation from ACTIVE, BLOCKED or AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER — 13568. Other status transitions and the agent combinations are not connected — see the overview.
Open questions / required validation
The exact behavior of adding and removing a customer in campaigns during future transitions needs a controlled check.

number

Use nowTemporary mode
API key and business meaning
Customer phone. Per knowledge confirmed by the user, CallMarker identifies the customer by number (this is not described in the API documentation).
Required / type
Required (per the documentation). String.
Mediwho source
users.phone (varchar(25), NOT NULL); for normalization the code also reads users.country_code (varchar(4), default '+972').
Transformation and sending rules
The final format needs verification, including for foreign numbers. The documentation describes two modes: international (number in E.164, e.g. +972501234567) and region (a local number such as 0501234567 plus region in ISO alpha-2, e.g. IL). The documentation does not say how the flags work together or which takes precedence. Non-resident status does not determine the phone country.
Documented API behavior
The number is interpreted according to international / region. The parsing rule without either flag is not documented.
Current implementation
normalizePhone() strips spaces, hyphens, dots, and parentheses, then:
  • a number starting with + or 00 is treated as international, validated (+972 as Israeli, otherwise 8–15 digits), and takes precedence over country_code; on a mismatch it emits the warning PHONE_COUNTRY_CODE_CONFLICT;
  • country_code 972: one leading 0 is dropped, a stored 972… prefix is allowed, and the national number is 9 digits (5x/7x) or 8 (landline);
  • a local number with another country code is rejected: PHONE_LOCAL_NON_IL_UNSUPPORTED; an invalid country_code gives PHONE_COUNTRY_CODE_INVALID;
  • empty country_code: only an unambiguous Israeli mobile 0?5XXXXXXXX is accepted, with the warning PHONE_COUNTRY_ASSUMED_IL; otherwise PHONE_COUNTRY_CODE_MISSING.
Temporary (TEMP, 2026-10-11): the callMarkerNumber function (shared by the customer test and Contact Us) sends Israeli numbers in local form, 0 + national number, without international and region (phone.sent_format: IL_LOCAL_TEMP). Foreign numbers are sent as E.164 + international=1. Phone errors return 422.
Live test result 2026-10-11
according to the test log in callmarker_instructions.md and the user reports: +972550824042 + international=1 → HTTP 200 with the body “Invalid number format”; local 0540824042 → the same response. As assessed in that session, the 054-0/055-0 ranges are not allocated in the masked dev data, so the rejection may have been caused by the number rather than the format. A valid Israeli number in local form without flags → “Customer has been added successfully”. The log has no re-check of E.164 + international=1 with a valid number.
Confirmed intended behavior
Source: users.phone (in Contact Us, the phone from the form or the app account). The number is the key by which CallMarker identifies the customer (confirmed by the user). Updating customer data through one campaign also changes the shared customer data in other campaigns (confirmed by the user). The final format and mode stay open until verified.
Open questions / required validation
Repeat E.164 + international=1 with a valid Israeli number. Find out what the parsing of a local number without region is based on. Check foreign numbers and the region mode. Then remove the temporary branch in callMarkerNumber or make it permanent. Open: phone change (a different number creates a new CRM row) and a number shared by family members (family behavior is deferred). The effect on statuses and agent assignments across campaigns has not been checked.

international

Decision pendingTemporary mode
API key and business meaning
Flag: the number is already in E.164 format.
Required / type
Optional flag (“if set”). The value is not specified; 1 is used.
Mediwho source
Integration setting (the selected phone mode), not customer data.
Transformation and sending rules
Send only in E.164 mode. Do not send an “if set” flag when it is not needed: it is unknown whether international=0 disables it.
Documented API behavior
If set, the customer number is treated as formatted in E.164.
Current implementation
international=1 is sent only for non-Israeli numbers. Such numbers can arrive only with an explicit +/00, because local non-Israeli numbers are rejected. For Israeli numbers the flag is temporarily not sent.
Live test result 2026-10-11
according to the test log in callmarker_instructions.md and the user reports: with the flag and the number +972550824042, “Invalid number format” (the number is probably from an unallocated range). Foreign numbers have not been checked.
Confirmed intended behavior
Candidate for the E.164 mode. Not confirmed, nor is its combined use with region.
Open questions / required validation
The accepted value; whether mere presence (international=) is enough; behavior together with region.

region

Decision pendingNot implemented
API key and business meaning
Country of the local number (ISO 3166-1 alpha-2).
Required / type
Optional. Two uppercase letters: IL, GB, US. A dialing code (972) is not valid.
Mediwho source
Mediwho does not store an ISO country. users.country_code is a dialing code (+972), and it does not map one-to-one to a country (+1 is US/CA, +7 is RU/KZ, +44 is GB/JE/GG/IM). Non-resident status is not the phone country.
Transformation and sending rules
Only in region mode: a local number together with the ISO code.
Documented API behavior
If set, the number can be sent in the local format of that region; CallMarker converts it to E.164 itself.
Current implementation
Never sent (explicitly listed under “Never sent” in callmarker_instructions.md).
Confirmed intended behavior
Candidate for the local-number mode. Combined use with international and precedence are not confirmed.
Open questions / required validation
Decide whether region mode is needed. Check a local number + region=IL and both flags together. Decide where to take the ISO country from.

ignore_duplicates

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Do not modify a customer that CallMarker already considers existing.
Required / type
Optional flag (“if set”). Contact Us sends 1.
Mediwho source
Set explicitly in the Contact Us fields; not added globally to all requests.
Transformation and sending rules
Contact Us: ignore_duplicates='1' in buildContactUsFields. The customer test does not send it.
Documented API behavior
If set and the customer already exists in the campaign, the response is “Duplicate customer” and the data is not updated. With duplicate_days, only customers who arrived within the last X days count as duplicates.
Current implementation
Sent only by Contact Us (app/Modules/CallMarker.js:217), not in sendCustomer. A response with “Duplicate customer” is classified as DUPLICATE and triggers an alert email. The customer test does not build it; it is not in CONTRACT_FIELDS.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Observed (the user report, 2026-10): with the flag, CallMarker responded “Duplicate customer” for a number that was already in a different campaign. Without the flag, a resend of the customer test updated the existing customer (test 4, per the test log and the user reports).
Confirmed intended behavior
Used only in Contact Us so that a lead does not change an existing customer. User sync never sends it (it checks the customer through API v2 first).
Open questions / required validation
The duplicate check is not necessarily limited to the target campaign; whether this applies to all campaign pairs and how duplicate_days affects it has not been established. The observation does not prove that a customer cannot belong to several campaigns. Use of the flag in future flows is a team decision.

5. Custom fields

Documented general rule for custom_field_{id}: Checkbox values must match a configured option or be zero; Select values must match a configured option. All fields are optional. IDs depend on the environment: dev and stage use the “(S)” IDs, production uses the original ones (both are given in the cards); env is the shared custom_field_16901 (env). The “(S)” sets separate only custom data: standard customer fields (name, number, email, crm_id, source), number matching and campaign membership are shared by all environments. IDs 16870–16872 were not provided; custom_field_16900 (insurance_question) exists in the account but is not used.

Agent resolution (as in Kaveret). The customer’s own agent if users.agent_id is set; otherwise the parent’s agent. Fields custom_field_16887 (agent_id) – custom_field_16891 (agent_mentor) describe this agent, and custom_field_16892 (parent_agency_id) – custom_field_16896 (parent_agency_mentor) describe the user found by its parent_agency_id. In the current code, CallMarker.loadCustomer does this with separate queries.

Mediwho source
users.birthday (DATE).
Transformation and sending rules
DD-MM-YYYY; API v2: YYYY-MM-DD.
Documented API behavior
The input format for Date custom fields is not documented. The follow-up/appointment date formats do not define it.
Current implementation
Fixed DD-MM-YYYY in the common mapper; no environment setting. Date uses the process-local calendar date; SQL YYYY-MM-DD / YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss strings are parsed strictly. Empty or invalid values are omitted, never clear the CRM field.

custom_field_16876 (active_product_id)

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
MediWho product ID (products.id) of the customer’s active subscription.
Required / type
Optional. Textfield. ID on dev/stage: custom_field_16959 (active_product_id(S)); in production — custom_field_16876 (active_product_id).
Mediwho source
Kaveret snapshot: the customer’s own ACTIVE user_subscriptions row → its product_subscriptions link with MIN(id) → the products row, only when products.active = 1.
Transformation and sending rules
As a string. No active subscription — skipped with EMPTY; two ACTIVE rows with different products — ambiguity SNAPSHOT_ROWS_DISAGREE.
Documented API behavior
General rule custom_field_{id}. For text fields, the format and length limits are not documented.
Current implementation
From the Kaveret snapshot (active_product_id = products.id); corrected on 2026-10-14, previously user_subscriptions.id. This is not the opportunity product_id, which is the internal CallMarker product ID.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Test 5 (2026-10-11) sent the previous value (user_subscriptions.id). The current source was not checked separately; the user confirmed the implemented flow on stage as a whole.
Confirmed intended behavior
products.id by the Kaveret rule (current decision; the earlier “user_subscriptions.id” was replaced).
Open questions / required validation
Whether family members covered by someone else’s subscription get a value (currently no). After a cancellation there is no active product: the field is omitted, not cleared, so the previous value may remain in the CRM; a clearing method is not agreed or tested.

custom_field_16879 (is_blocked)

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Whether the customer is covered by a subscription with BLOCKED or AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER status.
Required / type
Optional. Selectbox. ID on dev/stage: custom_field_16962 (is_blocked(S)); in production — custom_field_16879 (is_blocked).
Mediwho source
CallMarker’s own calculation (blockingSubscriptionsQuery in callMarkerCustomer.js, read in loadCustomer): a user_subscriptions row with BLOCKED or AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER status that the customer owns or in which the customer is listed in members.child / members.parent / members.partner. CARD_BLOCKED alone does not count. The is_blocked flag of the Kaveret snapshot (BLOCKED only) is no longer used.
Transformation and sending rules
Strings "1"/"0".
Documented API behavior
The value must exactly match an option from the field settings (documented for Select). The option list was not provided.
Current implementation
Sent on every full sync. The blocking, unblocking and cancellation contexts set the value explicitly: blocking — "1", unblocking and cancellation — "0". The CALLMARKER_IS_BLOCKED_MAP map is not used. No new family rule.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Test 5: not sent (SELECT_MAP_NOT_CONFIGURED). Blocking and unblocking have not been tested live.
Confirmed intended behavior
BLOCKED and AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER → "1"; a customer with a blocked subscription stays a customer.
Open questions / required validation
Whether the Selectbox accepts 1/0 — not verified. The flag does not drive blacklisted.

custom_field_16881 (created_at)

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
When the Mediwho user was created.
Required / type
Optional. Textfield. ID on dev/stage: custom_field_16964 (created_at(S)); in production — custom_field_16881 (created_at).
Mediwho source
users.created_at (DATETIME).
Transformation and sending rules
A text timestamp; the time-zone convention must be stated explicitly.
Documented API behavior
General rule custom_field_{id}. For text fields, the format and length limits are not documented.
Current implementation
Format YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss. config/database.js has no timezone option for the driver: it reads DATETIME in the process time zone, and moment writes the value back in the same zone. As a result, the stored “wall-clock” time is sent without a zone or offset. The zone it was written in is not documented (it depends on the TZ of the writing process), so CallMarker may interpret it as Israel time even though it is not. SQL strings in this format pass through unchanged; other strings give INVALID_DATE.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Test 5: the field was included in the request, and the request was accepted (“Customer has been added successfully”). The value was not separately checked in the CallMarker UI.
Confirmed intended behavior
users.created_at; the current format and the time-zone limitation are described here.
Open questions / required validation
Choose an explicit zone (e.g., conversion to Asia/Jerusalem) or an offset suffix; confirm the TZ of the database and containers.

The resolved agent, as in Kaveret: the customer’s own agent (users.agent_id), or, if that is NULL, the parent’s agent (parent.agent_id).

If there is no agent, the field is skipped with EMPTY; if the ID exists but the row does not, SOURCE_ROW_NOT_FOUND. The test checks inheritance of the agent from the parent and the precedence of the customer’s own agent.

CallMarker.loadCustomer resolves the agent (own agent_id, otherwise the parent’s agent) and reads its users row in a separate query. If there is no agent, the field is skipped with EMPTY; if the ID exists but the row does not, SOURCE_ROW_NOT_FOUND. fullName() joins the non-empty parts.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Test 5: the field was included in the request, and the request was accepted (“Customer has been added successfully”). The value was not separately checked in the CallMarker UI.
Confirmed intended behavior
first_name + last_name of the resolved agent; informational.
Open questions / required validation
—
