custom_field_16899 (roles)

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Computed Hebrew role and status labels; not the raw role slugs.
Required / type
Optional. Textfield (plain text). ID on dev/stage: custom_field_16982 (roles(S)); in production — custom_field_16899 (roles).
Mediwho source
Kaveret: roles.name of the user roles, except customer/subscriber; agent becomes מפיץ (and is dropped if the user is also an insurance_agent); plus one status label: לקוח (has an ACTIVE subscription, family included), otherwise לקוח לשעבר (CANCELED), otherwise רשום ללא מנוי.
Transformation and sending rules
Plain text: the labels in snapshot order joined with “, ”, without JSON brackets or quotes. Example: ["לקוח לשעבר"] → לקוח לשעבר; ["לקוח"] → לקוח; several labels are joined with “, ” (e.g. מפיץ, לקוח). Not an array of strings — INVALID_SHAPE; cannot be decoded — INVALID_JSON; empty — EMPTY.
Documented API behavior
General rule custom_field_{id}. For text fields, the format and length limits are not documented.
Current implementation
The agreed format is committed (98ecc453f, 2026-10-18, buildCustomerPayload in callMarkerCustomer.js); before that a compact JSON array was sent. Deployment and live verification are not confirmed (stage network access disabled by current policy). The calculation, order and Hebrew labels are unchanged. Taken from the shared Kaveret.scopeGetUserInfo snapshot. Several snapshot rows are compared by SNAPSHOT_KEYS (active_product_id, active_product_name, is_deleted, is_blocked, blocked_agent, roles; the set is not the set of sent fields): identical — the warning SNAPSHOT_DUPLICATE_ROWS_IDENTICAL; different — these fields are omitted as AMBIGUOUS, the user sync writes nothing (an AMBIGUOUS_SOURCE_DATA email), the customer test answers 409.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Historically (test 6, 2026-10-11): a JSON array was sent and the request was accepted; the user confirmed that the JSON with Hebrew labels is displayed. Plain text has not been tested live.
Confirmed intended behavior
Computed Hebrew labels as plain text joined with “, ”: production — custom_field_16899 (roles), dev/stage — custom_field_16982 (roles(S)).
Open questions / required validation
Verify the plain text on stage after deployment. The code has no 255 cap for roles. The actual CallMarker field limit is not established; truncation at 255 was observed once, on a 16897 (subscriptions) value.

Confirmed intended behavior
One ordinary lifecycle flag at a time; Contact Us preserves a more advanced or ambiguous classification.
Open questions / required validation
The lookup and the write are not atomic.

This is current implementation behavior, not a separately approved policy. Own (non-AGENT) subscriptions of users with their own agent role are handled the same way, plus is_agent = "1" and membership preservation; on recovery, family members are skipped.

A purchase or a recovery establishes the customer state.
Open questions / required validation

6. Opportunities

Opportunities are sent automatically in the same POST as the customer: on personal subscription activation — Purchased (campaign 13566), on cancellation — a separate Not purchased (status 3 with a reason ID, campaign 13568), on blocking — Not purchased with reason 367, on recovery and unblocking — a new Purchased with product_field_1060 (campaign 13566). Mapping: subscriptions.callmarker_product_id, user_subscriptions.subscription_id, user_subscriptions.price, status 2, users.callmarker_agent_name, product fields 1057/1058/1059. There is no persistent duplicate protection. Details, post-write verification and historical tests are in the overview; see example 3.
ParameterSourcePlanCode now
product_idsubscriptions.callmarker_product_id of the row’s tariff — a manually added column (BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL).Use nowCode matches decision
opportunity_crm_iduser_subscriptions.subscription_id (the tariff).Use nowCode matches decision
opportunity_priceuser_subscriptions.price — the stored subscription price, not necessarily the first charged amount.Use nowCode matches decision
opportunity_statusEvent: a purchase, recovery or unblocking (ACTIVE row), or a cancellation or blocking.Use nowCode matches decision
opportunity_agent_nameusers.callmarker_agent_name of the provided_by user — a manually added column (VARCHAR(255) NULL); trimmed.Use nowCode matches decision
opportunity_not_purchased_reason_idCancellation — the request_reason of the exact verified cancellation request by the 368–377 table, otherwise 378 (לא ידוע); blocking — 367 (חסימת מנוי).Use nowCode matches decision
product_field_{id}Provider, product and purchase row.Use nowCode matches decision

Parameter details

product_id

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Internal ID of the CallMarker catalog product for which the opportunity is created.
Required / type
Optional. CallMarker catalog product ID (string).
Mediwho source
subscriptions.callmarker_product_id of the row’s tariff — a manually added column (BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL).
Transformation and sending rules
A positive integer. Missing or invalid → PRODUCT_MAPPING_MISSING / PRODUCT_MAPPING_INVALID: no opportunity is sent, the customer is written without opportunity fields, and user 84 gets an email. No fallback (subscriptions.id, user_subscriptions.id, SKU, hardcoded IDs).
Documented API behavior
If set and there is no open opportunity for this product, a new opportunity is created. Without it, opportunity_crm_id has no effect.
Current implementation
Sent automatically in the same POST as the customer: on activation (campaign 13566), cancellation (13568, tariff of the canceled row), recovery, blocking and unblocking (13566, tariff of the event row).
Live test result 2026-10-11
Historical tests: the internal ID 7794 created an opportunity; user_subscriptions.id, subscriptions.id and the products-table row number did not (200 without an opportunity).
Confirmed intended behavior
Internal CallMarker product ID. The SQL update artifact supplied 137 “tariff → product” mappings (example: subscriptions.id 192 / SKU I-0004 → 7794); the documentation did not query or verify them individually.
Open questions / required validation
The ID belongs to the catalog of the configured CallMarker account (with a shared catalog, stage and production may use the same IDs). The product must be active, have an open price and be configured for campaign 13566, where the automatic purchase goes. Browser inspection confirmed that 7794 is associated with test campaign 13565; association with 13566 is not explicitly confirmed — that campaign’s configuration must be checked.

opportunity_crm_id

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
External MediWho identifier in the opportunity properties.
Required / type
Optional. String. Takes effect only together with product_id.
Mediwho source
user_subscriptions.subscription_id (the tariff).
Transformation and sending rules
As a string.
Documented API behavior
Added to the opportunity properties; takes effect only when product_id is set.
Current implementation
Sent automatically with the opportunity.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Duplicate opportunity_crm_id values did not prevent duplicates.
Confirmed intended behavior
Identifies the tariff, not a purchase: every purchase of the same tariff has the same value. Not an idempotency key. The specific purchase is linked by product_field_1059.
Open questions / required validation
—

opportunity_price

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Opportunity price.
Required / type
Optional. Decimal number with a dot.
Mediwho source
user_subscriptions.price — the stored subscription price, not necessarily the first charged amount.
Transformation and sending rules
As stored: zero and decimals are kept; an invalid value is the PRICE_INVALID error, never coerced.
Documented API behavior
Applied only if the related CallMarker product has an open price.
Current implementation
Sends user_subscriptions.price as stored (not the payment amount). If the product price is not open, CallMarker silently uses the catalog price — post-write verification reports the mismatch.
Live test result 2026-10-11
The price applied only after the product’s open price was enabled; the value 90 was verified.
Confirmed intended behavior
user_subscriptions.price.
Open questions / required validation
Whether every mapped product has an open price.

opportunity_status

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Opportunity status on write.
Required / type
Optional. 2 — Purchased, 3 — Not purchased; omitted — open or update.
Mediwho source
Event: a purchase, recovery or unblocking (ACTIVE row), or a cancellation or blocking.
Transformation and sending rules
Purchase, recovery and unblocking — "2"; cancellation and blocking — "3".
Documented API behavior
Omitted: the opportunity is opened or updated; 2 — Purchased; 3 — Not purchased.
Current implementation
"2" on purchase, recovery and unblocking; "3" on cancellation and blocking — as a separate new Not purchased opportunity from the exact event row, with a reason ID. The existing Purchased opportunity is not changed.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Direct creation as Purchased works. Opened → Purchased and Opened → Not purchased updated the same open record. status 2 or 3 without an open opportunity created another closed record.
Confirmed intended behavior
Purchase, recovery and unblocking → 2; cancellation and blocking → 3 as a separate record. Status 3 is not a cancellation of an existing sale.
Open questions / required validation
If an open opportunity exists for the product, or its absence cannot be established, cancellation, recovery, blocking and unblocking do not send opportunity fields (overview).

opportunity_agent_name

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
CallMarker agent credited with the opportunity.
Required / type
Optional; documented as required when closing. String.
Mediwho source
users.callmarker_agent_name of the provided_by user — a manually added column (VARCHAR(255) NULL); trimmed.
Transformation and sending rules
Sent only when non-empty; the MediWho name is never substituted.
Documented API behavior
Agent name as it exists in CallMarker, or the external CRM Name configured for the agent.
Current implementation
Empty — not sent (AGENT_NAME_NOT_CONFIGURED). After the write the agent is judged only from the returned opportunity: CONFIRMED (the returned Name equals the configured one), AGENT_NOT_ASSIGNED (no user — email), IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED (another Name; the configured value may be a CRM Name — the agent is not proven correct), NOT_SENT.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Live matching was confirmed for the Name “Yuval Kopel REP” (CallMarker agent 13619). Unknown or unresolved names did not prevent closing but left the assignment empty. CRM Name matching was not tested live.
Confirmed intended behavior
Configured manually for the provider: the exact CallMarker user Name or that user’s configured CRM Name.
Open questions / required validation
Creating Purchased without opportunity_agent_name was not verified by a live test. CRM Name matching and agent identity verification are limited: API v2 exposes only the user id and Name.

opportunity_not_purchased_reason_id

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Reason for Not purchased.
Required / type
Optional. ID of a reason configured in the CallMarker account.
Mediwho source
Cancellation — the request_reason of the exact verified cancellation request by the 368–377 table, otherwise 378 (לא ידוע); blocking — 367 (חסימת מנוי).
Transformation and sending rules
As a string; only together with opportunity_status = "3".
Documented API behavior
May be provided when closing the opportunity as Not purchased.
Current implementation
Blocking — "367"; cancellation — the ID of the request’s reason, otherwise "378". The request comes only from the event and must reference this subscription row and its owner (requested_for); the latest request is never substituted. Manual cancellation passes no request → 378. A failed request read → 378 with the diagnostic CANCELLATION_REQUEST_READ_FAILED; the customer and opportunity sync continues. UPGRADED is not an alias for UPGRADE → 378. Purchase, recovery and unblocking send no reason.
Live test result 2026-10-11
Not verified: Simple API acceptance of IDs 367–378 and their return through API v2 are not confirmed.
Confirmed intended behavior
The reason table is in the overview. The IDs are one account catalog, independent of the environment, and are not custom field IDs.
Open questions / required validation
Live verification pending (stage network access disabled by current policy): whether 367–378 (except the confirmed 371, including 378) are accepted and returned through the API v2 field not_purchased_reason_id.

product_field_{id}

Use nowCode matches decision
API key and business meaning
Opportunity product fields: 1057, 1058, 1059; 1060 — recovery or unblocking note; 1061 — collection note on cancellation from BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER.
Required / type
Optional. Text.
Mediwho source
Provider, product and purchase row.
Transformation and sending rules
product_field_1057 — the provider’s “first last - users.id” (first_name + last_name + “ - ” + users.id), plain text without a link, independent of callmarker_agent_name; product_field_1058 — products.name of the purchased tariff by the Kaveret rule (MIN(product_subscriptions.id), products.active = 1); product_field_1059 — user_subscriptions.id; product_field_1060 — שחזור מנוי on recovery and הוסדר תשלום on unblocking; product_field_1061 — the msg of the first message of the exact verified request, only on cancellation from BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER with reason RETURNED_CHARGE, DENIAL_OF_TRANSACTION or BLOCKING_PAYMENT_METHOD, text as stored; otherwise not sent.
Documented API behavior
Product field with a unique ID, e.g. product_field_123.
Current implementation
Sent automatically with the opportunity (purchase, cancellation, recovery, blocking, unblocking). A missing provider name or product name only omits that value (PROVIDER_NAME_EMPTY, PRODUCT_NAME_UNRESOLVED).
Live test result 2026-10-11
The values 1057/1058/1059 persisted through closing and are readable through API v2 (historical test). Confirmed by the user on stage: a recovery creates a new Purchased opportunity with the recovery note.
Confirmed intended behavior
product_field_1059 links the opportunity to the subscription row; it is a correlation, not a unique event key: all opportunities of the same row (purchase, cancellation, recovery, blocking, unblocking) share its value.
Open questions / required validation
—

8. Inbound messages (deferred)

Endpoint POST|GET https://app.callmarker.com/api/simple/customers/messages; request — form-data or JSON; response — string or JSON. The whole block is deferred. The endpoint records a message in CallMarker; the provided documentation does not indicate that it sends or delivers anything to the customer. The source does not name the direction parameter, so its key is marked as unknown.

9. Risks and discrepancies in the current code

Based on reading the files on 2026-10-11, after the parallel session’s edits. Fixes were not taken on trust: every item below was verified against the code.

AreaCurrent codeExpectation (documentation / decision)Consequence
Phone (TEMP)callMarkerNumber (shared by all flows): Israeli numbers are sent in local form without international/region; foreign numbers as E.164 + international=1; local non-Israeli numbers are rejected.No mode chosen; neither of the two documented modes is used for Israel.The main phone description in callmarker_instructions.md still refers to E.164 + international=1. The E.164 rejection is proven only for a number from an unallocated range.
Opportunities without duplicate protectionPurchase, cancellation, recovery, blocking and unblocking opportunities are sent automatically without persistent duplicate protection (an explicit decision by the user).Deferred by the user, as is payment idempotency.Repeated or concurrent events, two pods during a rollout or a manual resend after a timeout can create duplicates. product_field_1059 helps find them but does not prevent them; all opportunities of the same row share it. Repeated sends can create additional closed opportunities.
Active product after cancellationcustom_field_16876 (active_product_id) and custom_field_16877 (active_product_name) are omitted after a cancellation, not cleared.A clearing method is not agreed.The previous product may remain in the CRM.
Moving between campaignsA sync write with another campaign moves the customer (test 2026-10-14).Multi-campaign operations are needed.The customer may leave the previous campaign (a test observation, not a general contract); agents’ own subscriptions are now written with membership preservation verified after the write (no unique, repair or retry).
custom_field_16873 (passport)Any non-NULL value is sent as a string.Schema in the repository: tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0."0"/"1" may be sent instead of a passport number.
custom_field_16886 (parent_related_as)The placeholder לא הוזן is sent if related_as is not recognized.The Kaveret translations are confirmed; there is no explicit decision about the placeholder.The CRM receives the text “not entered”.
custom_field_16881 (created_at)YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss without a time zone.Current format with the described limitation.Ambiguous time.
Active subscriptionTwo own ACTIVE rows with different products — diverging snapshot rows, the send is blocked.The “one active subscription” invariant is a business rule.Family members without their own subscription get empty 16876/16877.
Shared Kaveret snapshotUsed for active_product_id/name, is_deleted, is_blocked, blocked_agent and roles.The shared SQL is not changed.The defective JOIN can still multiply rows (see section 10).
CallMarker responsesHTTP 200 and “Customer has been added successfully” do not confirm the opportunity, its price, status or agent.Post-write verification is read-only through API v2.A timeout leaves the result undetermined; there are no retries.
Example env fileThere is no .env.example; placeholders are in callmarker_instructions.md.—Variables are set manually (Azure Key Vault for stage).
Tests and lintMock tests I ran on 2026-10-15 (without network, DB or .env): Contact Us — 34, customer test — 43, user sync — 67, subscription activation — 17; all passed. ESLint was not run in this task; the last known error is the unused isCard in KaveretController.js (2026-10-14).—Mock tests do not prove compatibility with the CRM.

Fixed compared with the previous code version

  • campaign is sent instead of campaign_id; the test campaign is the constant CAMPAIGNS_LIST.TESTS_CAMPAIGN (13565).
  • The route has auth + is:(admin or developer) protection (commit ac3ecdd09, 2026-10-12); on 2026-10-11, in the uncommitted version, it was commented out.
  • The code is consolidated into three main files: CallMarkerController.js, app/Modules/CallMarker.js, app/Utils/callMarkerCustomer.js.
  • region is no longer sent together with E.164.
  • HMO and HMO plan: Hebrew catalog names only; unknown IDs omitted. Birthday: fixed DD-MM-YYYY; fathers_name: own user, trimmed (17022 / 17023). Stage confirmed by user, 2026-10-26; production unverified.
  • custom_field_16876 (active_product_id) / custom_field_16877 (active_product_name) — products.id / products.name per the Kaveret rule (since 2026-10-14).
  • Custom field IDs are chosen by environment: “(S)” on dev/stage, the original ones in production; env is the shared custom_field_16901 (env).
  • custom_field_16878 (is_deleted) and custom_field_16879 (is_blocked) are sent as "1"/"0"; option maps are no longer used.
  • custom_field_16897 (subscriptions) is no longer sent; custom_field_16898 (address) is readable text with a provisional 255-code-point integration cap.
  • The Purchased opportunity is sent automatically with the internal CallMarker product ID (subscriptions.callmarker_product_id) and is verified by reading after the write.
  • An ordinary ACTIVE → CANCELED cancellation (manual, Galil, scheduled) and a CANCELED → ACTIVE recovery are synced with separate Not purchased and Purchased opportunities (commits 2282e8548 and 04b1126a5, 2026-10-18); the collective purchase is connected to SUBSCRIPTION_ACTIVATED.
  • Blocking and unblocking are synced (SUBSCRIPTION_BLOCKED / SUBSCRIPTION_UNBLOCKED), cancellation covers BLOCKED and AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER, Not purchased opportunities get a reason ID (367–378), and a cancellation from a blocked state gets the product_field_1061 note (commits 98ecc453f and dabf47e63, 2026-10-18); custom_field_16899 (roles) is sent as plain text.
  • The agent and agency are read with separate queries (CallMarker.loadCustomer); the defective JOIN is not used for agency fields.
  • Ambiguous data blocks the send; the shape of the raw result is validated (readRawRows).
  • All values are sent as strings; CONTRACT_FIELDS describes the contract and is verified by a test, but does not filter fields at runtime.

10. Agency JOIN defect in the shared snapshot

The shared Kaveret query app/Models/Kaveret.js (scopeGetUserInfo) is unchanged and contains:

LEFT JOIN users parent_agent ON agent.parent_agency_id = parent.id      -- line 235
...
CASE WHEN u.parent_agency_id IS NOT NULL THEN JSON_OBJECT('id', parent_agent.id, ...) -- lines 87-96
  • The ON condition does not reference parent_agent at all. If agent.parent_agency_id = parent.id is false or NULL, parent_agent is NULL. If it is true, every users row matches: the snapshot is multiplied by the size of the entire users table.
  • Wrong trigger. The object is built from the customer’s own u.parent_agency_id rather than the agent’s; inherited agents (agent_of_parent) are not taken into account.
  • A second source of duplicates. LEFT JOIN user_subscriptions us_active … status = 'ACTIVE' repeats the user for each of their own ACTIVE rows.
  • What CallMarker does now. The agent/parent_agency and hmo objects of the snapshot are not used: the agent, the agency and users.hmo_id are read by CallMarker.loadCustomer with separate queries. In the general customer mapping only custom_field_16876 (active_product_id), custom_field_16877 (active_product_name), custom_field_16878 (is_deleted), custom_field_16880 (blocked_agent; agents only) and the labels of custom_field_16899 (roles) come from the snapshot. Loaded or calculated separately: custom_field_16879 (is_blocked) — blockingSubscriptionsQuery; custom_field_16898 (address) — from the addresses rows; the opportunity field product_field_1058 — by its own purchase loading rule (loadPurchase). custom_field_16897 (subscriptions) is not sent (NOT_USED).
  • Duplicate snapshot rows. pickSnapshot compares rows by SNAPSHOT_KEYS: active_product_id, active_product_name, is_deleted, is_blocked, blocked_agent, roles. This set is not the set of sent fields: the snapshot is_blocked (BLOCKED only) is not sent to CallMarker but still takes part in the comparison, and blocked_agent is compared for non-agents too. Identical rows are collapsed with the warning SNAPSHOT_DUPLICATE_ROWS_IDENTICAL. If rows disagree, all six keys are omitted as AMBIGUOUS: the user sync writes nothing and sends an AMBIGUOUS_SOURCE_DATA email, the customer test answers 409. Duplicates from the agency JOIN do not change these keys; two own ACTIVE subscriptions with different products produce disagreeing rows.
  • Residual risk. When the condition is “true”, the query returns as many rows as there are users in the table. This puts load on the DB and memory, although the data is correct. Kaveret, for its part, sends all rows to its webhook.

Correct agency source: users agency ON agency.id = resolved_agent.parent_agency_id — this is what CallMarker.loadCustomer does. The shared SQL is intentionally left unchanged because Kaveret depends on it. Fixing it is a separate task with its own Kaveret regression testing.

11. Campaign reference

The catalog is CAMPAIGNS_LIST in app/Constants/Enums.js; IDs are stored as strings. The “# in CSV” column is the position in the campaigns.csv export, not the ID (in the export, Min. Calls = 30, Time Between = 180 and status Active for all; units are not specified). Detailed transition rules are in the summary.

KeyIDName# in CSVUsed in code nowApproved rule
LEADS_CAMPAIGN13558לידים1Contact Us in productionContact Us (confirmed)
TESTS_CAMPAIGN13565בדיקות2Customer test always; Contact Us on stage; historical manual opportunity testsTests
CUSTOMERS_CAMPAIGN13566לקוחות3Personal subscription activation (SUBSCRIPTION_ACTIVATED, including the collective purchase) recovery (SUBSCRIPTION_RECOVERED), blocking and unblocking (SUBSCRIPTION_BLOCKED / SUBSCRIPTION_UNBLOCKED): customer and a separate opportunitySubscription became ACTIVE (purchase, recovery from CANCELED, unblocking) or was blocked
AGENTS_CAMPAIGN13567סוכנים4Agent registration and agent addressAgent registration
FORMER_CUSTOMERS_CAMPAIGN13568לקוחות לשעבר5ACTIVE / BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER → CANCELED cancellation (SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELED): customer and a separate Not purchased opportunity with a reason IDCancellation: manual, Galil, scheduled future cancellation execution (from ACTIVE only)
REGISTERED_WITHOUT_SUBSCRIPTION13569רשום ללא מנוי6User registration on the website and by an agentOrdinary user registration

Not all transitions are connected. Cancellation writes to 13568 only from ACTIVE, BLOCKED and AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER through three connected paths (manual cancellation, Galil, scheduled future cancellation execution; the scheduled cancellation handles ACTIVE only); CARD_BLOCKED and AWAITING_BANK_APPROVAL → CANCELED are not connected. The connected blocking (ACTIVE → BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER, CANCELED → BLOCKED) and unblocking transitions write to 13566. Manual activation of other statuses and expiry are not connected; subscription contexts are skipped for users with their own agent role (the agent combinations are not implemented). A write with another campaign moves the customer (test 2026-10-14). The current membership can be read through API v2 (overview), but a write does not preserve it. Statuses that are not synced (for example, CARD_BLOCKED, EXPIRED) keep the previous membership, so a campaign cannot be derived from a single current status snapshot.

12. Request examples with explanations

Only fictitious data and secret placeholders. Example 1 shows the body the current code would build; this exact set was not sent. Similar requests with other data passed in the live test.

13. Manual verification checklist

Only the test campaign (בדיקות, 13565), a dedicated test user and a manually set token. Record every response verbatim, without the token. The “done” marks are taken from the 2026-10-11 live test log; the checkboxes are working notes and are not saved.

Transport and authorization

  • A form-urlencoded POST is accepted; the exact success response text is recorded.✓ done: “Customer has been added successfully”
  • The token parameter in the body is accepted; API v2 is read with a separate Bearer token.✓ done
  • Response for a missing or invalid token.
  • auth + is:(admin or developer) protection on the customer test route.✓ done: in the code since the 2026-10-12 commit

Implemented flow on stage

  • The deployed flow was verified on stage.✓ done: confirmed by the user (individual cases and IDs were not provided)
  • Verification in production.

Contact Us

  • Website: validation on stage.✓ done: confirmed by the user
  • +60 app: separate controlled validation on stage (lead, campaign 13565, source, empty last name, review account).
  • ignore_duplicates=1: “Duplicate customer” response for a number from another campaign.✓ observed (user report)
  • The alert email to user 84 actually arrives (sending the event does not prove delivery).

Campaigns

  • A write with another campaign moves the customer.✓ observed: test 2026-10-14
  • Updating data through one campaign changes the customer’s shared data in other campaigns.✓ confirmed by the user
  • How to add a customer to a second campaign without moving it (a question for CallMarker support).
  • Effect of unique on the agents campaign; agent statuses and assignments across campaigns.

Lifecycle checkboxes

  • fields[customers] with include=custom_fields&custom_fields_format=map; a ticked checkbox is "1", an unticked one is null.✓ done: the user’s report
  • Sending "0" unticks the checkbox.✓ done: the user’s report
  • The three steps “lead → registered → customer” preserve the production and agent checkboxes, the test marks and env.✓ done: the user’s report
  • Cancellation: campaign 13568, is_former_customer = "1", the other three — "0", is_agent and is_test unchanged.✓ done: confirmed by the user on stage (not all entry points)

Phone modes

  • Israel, local form without flags (current TEMP).✓ done: a valid number was accepted
  • Israel, E.164 + international, with a valid number.
  • Local number + region=IL; both flags together.
  • Foreign numbers (for example, GB, US, IT — the leading-zero case).
  • Empty country_code; a non-resident with an Israeli number.

birthday format

  • Verify production birthday 16869 with DD-MM-YYYY after deployment; stage 16955 is confirmed.
  • Behavior with an invalid format.

Selectbox options

  • Selectbox accepts the option text: “dev” in custom_field_16901 (env) is stored and displayed.✓ done: test 7
  • The “stage” and “prod” option texts in custom_field_16901 (env).
  • Whether custom_field_16878 (is_deleted) and custom_field_16879 (is_blocked), including the “(S)” versions, accept the values 1/0.

Text values

  • The leading zero in custom_field_16868 (identity) is preserved in the UI (the code does this, verified by a mock test; not compared in the CRM).
  • Display of custom_field_16881 (created_at) and the chosen time zone.
  • The actual CallMarker field length limits for custom_field_16898 (address; provisional 255-code-point integration cap) and custom_field_16899 (roles; no cap in the code).
  • custom_field_16899 (roles) as plain text after deployment.

Customer duplicates

  • Re-sending with the same number without flags.✓ done: record updated, no duplicate (test 4)
  • The customer is identified by number; a different number with the same crm_id creates a new CRM row.✓ confirmed by the user
  • Business decisions: phone change, a number shared within a family.

Clearing fields

  • Whether an omitted field keeps its previous value.
  • Whether an empty value clears it.

Opportunities

  • The internal CallMarker product ID is required.✓ done: historical test
  • Direct creation as Purchased.✓ done: historical test
  • The exact Name “Yuval Kopel REP” → CallMarker agent 13619; an unknown name does not prevent closing, but the assignment is empty.✓ done: historical test
  • Opened → Purchased and Opened → Not purchased update the same open opportunity; without an open opportunity, status 2 or 3 creates a new closed one.✓ done: historical test
  • A repeated opportunity_crm_id does not prevent duplicates; fields 1057/1058/1059 are preserved on closing.✓ done: historical test
  • A cancellation creates a new Not purchased opportunity; previous opportunities are not changed.✓ done: confirmed by the user on stage
  • A recovery creates a new Purchased opportunity with product_field_1060; previous opportunities are not changed.✓ done: confirmed by the user on stage
  • Collective purchase on stage: 13566, is_customer, Purchased with the purchase price, product_field_1059 = the new user_subscriptions.id.
  • Reason IDs 367–378 are accepted and returned through API v2 (not_purchased_reason_id), including 378.
  • Manual cancellation sends 378; a request-linked cancellation sends its reason’s ID.
  • Blocking: 13566, is_blocked = 1, Not purchased with reason 367; unblocking: is_blocked = 0, Purchased with the note הוסדר תשלום.
  • Cancellation from BLOCKED / AWAITING_BANK_TRANSFER: 13568, is_blocked = 0, the correct product_field_1061.
  • Recovery keeps the note שחזור מנוי; previous opportunities are not changed in any of these scenarios.
  • Creating Purchased without opportunity_agent_name.
  • CRM Name matching (API v2 cannot confirm identity: only id and Name).
  • Persistent protection against duplicate opportunities.

Responses

  • Collect the observed response texts: “Customer has been added successfully”, “Invalid number format”, “Duplicate customer” and others. The response to test 7 was not recorded.✓ partially
  • HTTP 200 by itself does not prove creation; verify the opportunity through API v2.

14. Unresolved questions

  1. Contact Us from the +60 app: live verification pending; stage network access disabled by current policy (code reviewed and covered by mock tests).
  2. Phone mode: keep or remove the temporary local form for Israel; repeat E.164 + international with a valid number; decide on region and foreign numbers.
  3. env: replacing top-level env with custom_field_16901 (env) is approved, the “dev” option is confirmed by test 7; the “stage” and “prod” option texts remain to be verified.
  4. Phone change and shared number: the customer is identified by number; a different number creates a new CRM row. Family member behavior — deferred.
  5. Storing campaign membership and sync state — deferred. For Contact Us the error policy is decided: an alert email without retries, limitations accepted.
  6. The /new-client flow: whether to connect CallMarker to it and who generates the link.
  7. Campaign transitions (partially implemented): other status transitions, manual activation of other statuses and agents with a personal subscription are not connected; how to add a customer to a second campaign without moving it is a question for CallMarker support; unique can remove the agents campaign.
  8. Standard agent_name: mapping for agent assignment.
  9. unique / ignore_duplicates: ignore_duplicates is used explicitly in Contact Us; “Duplicate customer” was observed for a number from another campaign. The meaning of unique and the use of the flags in future flows are not approved. There are no inclusion rules for duplicate_days, remove and attachment.
  10. custom_field_16869 (birthday) — production verification of DD-MM-YYYY (stage confirmed); custom_field_16878 (is_deleted) / custom_field_16879 (is_blocked) — whether the Selectbox accepts the values 1/0; custom_field_16881 (created_at) — time zone.
  11. custom_field_16893 (parent_agency_name): confirm the mapping.
  12. custom_field_16873 (passport): verify the actual column type.
  13. custom_field_16886 (parent_related_as): whether to send the placeholder לא הוזן.
  14. Active subscription for family members (custom_field_16876 (active_product_id) / custom_field_16877 (active_product_name)).
  15. custom_field_16899 (roles): plain text is in the API code since 2026-10-18; deployment and live verification pending; stage network access disabled by current policy.
  16. Length limits for custom_field_16897 (subscriptions), custom_field_16898 (address) and custom_field_16899 (roles); custom_field_16898 (address) was not sent in the tests.
  17. Parent and agency fields (custom_field_16882 (parent_id) – custom_field_16886 (parent_related_as), custom_field_16892 (parent_agency_id), custom_field_16894 (parent_agency_phone) – custom_field_16896 (parent_agency_mentor)): empty for the test user, not verified.
  18. Opportunities (overview): persistent duplicate protection and payment idempotency — deferred by the user; acceptance of reason IDs 367–378 and the product_field_1061 note are not tested live; blocking and unblocking are not verified on stage; clearing custom_field_16876 (active_product_id) / custom_field_16877 (active_product_name) after a cancellation is unresolved; the collective purchase is not verified on stage; creating Purchased without opportunity_agent_name is not verified; the rule for replacing unclear checkboxes on purchase is the current code behavior, not separately approved.
  19. Messages: the name of the direction key. Appointments and messages are deferred entirely.
  20. Clearing fields and a complete catalog of CallMarker responses.
